    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');

class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Lovable Cloud" data-page-href="/features/cloud" id="content"><span data-as="p">Lovable <a class="link" href="/features/hosting">hosts your app</a> and includes a built-in backend (Cloud) with a database, storage, authentication, realtime, and functions. Describe what you want directly in Lovable, and Lovable generates both the frontend and backend, ready to run. Instead of managing hardware or complex infrastructure, you get on-demand access to hosting, compute, and storage that scale automatically as your app grows.</span>
<span data-as="p">The built-in backend utilizes Supabase’s open-source foundation, which means you get a production-ready environment from day one, without needing to set up Supabase separately: a database, real-time updates, user authentication, and storage, with none of the manual setup steps. Across the docs, this is called the <strong>built-in backend (Cloud)</strong>, as opposed to an external backend you connect yourself, such as <a class="link" href="/integrations/supabase">Supabase</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="why-use-the-built-in-backend"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#why-use-the-built-in-backend" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Why use the built-in backend</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Three things make it the default choice for most projects:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Scales as you grow</strong>: From your first prototype to millions of users, Lovable scales seamlessly with your needs.</li>
<li><strong>Secure by default</strong>: Enterprise-grade authentication, fine-grained access controls, and compliance best practices protect your app and data.</li>
<li><strong>Cost-effective</strong>: Manage multiple projects in one workspace with credit-based usage. Lovable includes monthly Cloud grants on Free, Pro, and Business plans. For details, see <a class="link" href="/introduction/credits-and-usage">Credits and usage</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="what’s-included"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#what’s-included" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">What’s included</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">You’ll find the <strong>Cloud</strong> tools inside any Lovable project by opening the <a class="link" href="/features/projects/editor#the-more-menu"><strong>More</strong> tab</a> in the project toolbar and selecting <strong>Cloud</strong>.</span>
<span data-as="p">Each area has its own page with full details, in the order they appear in the Cloud view:</span>

<span data-as="p">Your app’s <a class="link" href="/features/ai">AI features</a> have their own <strong>AI</strong> section in the <strong>More</strong> menu, outside Cloud.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="enabling-the-built-in-backend-cloud"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#enabling-the-built-in-backend-cloud" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Enabling the built-in backend (Cloud)</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">By default, the built-in backend (Cloud) is enabled for your workspace. When a feature you ask for requires backend functionality (for example, authentication or file storage), Lovable enables Cloud automatically or asks for your approval in chat first, depending on the <strong>Enable Cloud</strong> permission described below.</span>
<span data-as="p">Lovable can’t turn Cloud on from inside a <a class="link" href="/features/drafts">draft</a>. Ask in the main version of your project instead, then continue in the draft once Cloud is on.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">On some Enterprise workspaces, the built-in backend (Cloud) is disabled at the workspace level, and projects show “Lovable Cloud has been disabled by your workspace admin”. This setting is not self-serve; contact your Lovable account team to change it.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="cloud-permissions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#cloud-permissions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Cloud permissions</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Each Cloud tool has three permission options:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Always allow</strong>: Lovable automatically performs the action, without asking for review or approval. This is the default for Cloud.</li>
<li><strong>Ask each time</strong>: Lovable asks for your approval whenever the action is needed.</li>
<li><strong>Never allow</strong>: Lovable blocks the action, informs you that Cloud access is required, and instructs you to enable Cloud.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="tool-settings-and-defaults"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#tool-settings-and-defaults" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Tool settings and defaults</span></h3>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_4kllktbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px] [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums"><thead><tr><th>Setting</th><th>Description</th><th>Default behavior</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Enable Cloud</td><td>Create and connect a Cloud project to add backend features (database, auth, storage). If Cloud is auto-enabled using Always allow, this region is used automatically.</td><td>Region closest to your location</td></tr><tr><td>Read database</td><td>Allow Lovable to read your database.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Read analytics</td><td>Query logs to investigate issues and behavior.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Run security checks</td><td>Run automated security checks on your database. Not a full audit.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Modify database</td><td>Make schema and policy changes via SQL migrations. Updates generated types.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Add data</td><td>Add data to your database tables.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Read project info</td><td>Read project configuration and environment details.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Configure auth</td><td>Allow Lovable to configure authentication settings (for example, signup policies).</td><td>Always allow</td></tr><tr><td>Read backend logs</td><td>Read logs from backend services.</td><td>Always allow</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">You can change your Cloud tool preferences anytime: open <strong>Connectors</strong>, select <strong>Cloud</strong>, and adjust the settings under <strong>Manage my agent’s permissions</strong>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="region-selection"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#region-selection" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Region selection</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Projects can be hosted in three regions:</span>
<ul>
<li>Americas</li>
<li>Europe</li>
<li>Asia Pacific</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">You can set a preferred default region for new Cloud projects under <strong>Where projects are hosted</strong> in the same permissions view. If no preferred region is set, Lovable automatically preselects the region closest to your current location, and you can change it before confirming.</span>
<span data-as="p">Choosing a region closer to your users results in:</span>
<ul>
<li>Lower latency</li>
<li>Faster database responses</li>
<li>Region-based data hosting</li>
<li>Better overall backend performance</li>
</ul>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">After Cloud is enabled, you cannot change the selected region, and you cannot move existing projects between regions.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">To see which region an existing project is hosted in, go to <strong>Cloud tab → Overview → Advanced settings → General → Project location</strong>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="workspace-level-default-hosting-region"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#workspace-level-default-hosting-region" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Workspace-level default hosting region</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">On <strong>Business</strong> and <strong>Enterprise</strong> workspaces, admins can set a required default region for every new project from <a href="https://lovable.dev/settings/privacy-security" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Privacy &amp; security</a> (<strong>Settings → Security → Privacy &amp; security → Default hosting region</strong>). When a default hosting region is set, workspace members cannot choose a different region when creating a project. The setting applies only to new projects. Existing projects keep the region they were created in.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Country-specific regions</strong> (for example, Germany, Japan, or United States East) are available on Enterprise plans by request. Contact your Lovable account team to enable them for your workspace.</span>
<span data-as="p">See <a class="link" href="/features/privacy-and-security-settings#default-hosting-region">Default hosting region</a> for more information.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="tutorial-build-a-crm-by-prompting"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#tutorial-build-a-crm-by-prompting" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Tutorial: build a CRM by prompting</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">This walkthrough builds a real app, a customer relationship manager (CRM), entirely by prompting. Each step introduces one backend capability and shows the kind of prompt that activates it.</span>

<span data-as="p">Services like Stripe or Resend give you private API keys. Lovable prompts you to add them securely whenever they’re needed, and only your app can use them. Tools that sign in with an account instead of a key, such as Google services, connect through <a class="link" href="/integrations/introduction">Connectors</a>.</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="cloud-and-ai-gateway-usage-costs"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#cloud-and-ai-gateway-usage-costs" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Cloud and AI gateway usage costs</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Lovable uses one credit balance for building your app, hosting it and running its built-in backend (Cloud), and AI features in deployed apps. Cloud and AI gateway usage is measured as part of <strong>Run credits</strong>.</span>
<span data-as="p">See <a class="link" href="/features/project-usage">Project usage</a> for the per-project usage breakdown and monthly grants, and <a class="link" href="/introduction/credits-and-usage">Credits and usage</a> for how credits work across your workspace.</span>

What does this change mean for me?</p></div></summary><div id="i-m-currently-using-the-supabase-integration-what-does-this-change-mean-for-me-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="i-m-currently-using-the-supabase-integration-what-does-this-change-mean-for-me-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Don’t worry, nothing changes for your existing projects. Your Supabase integration will continue to work exactly as it does today, and we will continue to support it.</span><ul>
<li>For existing projects: continue using Supabase with no changes.</li>
<li>For new projects: choose either the built-in backend (Cloud) or Supabase.</li>
</ul></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-change-my-mind-after-adding-cloud-to-my-project-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-change-my-mind-after-adding-cloud-to-my-project" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-change-my-mind-after-adding-cloud-to-my-project-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I change my mind after adding Cloud to my project?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-change-my-mind-after-adding-cloud-to-my-project-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-change-my-mind-after-adding-cloud-to-my-project-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Yes. If you no longer want a project to use the built-in backend (Cloud), go to <strong>Cloud tab → Overview → Advanced settings</strong> and use <a class="link" href="/features/advanced-settings#remove-lovable-cloud">Remove Lovable Cloud</a>.</span><span data-as="p">This permanently deletes your Cloud instance and cannot be undone. Export your database and download any storage files you need before continuing.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-remix-cloud-projects-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-remix-cloud-projects" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-remix-cloud-projects-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I remix Cloud projects?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-remix-cloud-projects-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-remix-cloud-projects-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Yes, Cloud projects can be remixed to Cloud projects only. This creates a copy of your project that you can edit separately in your currently selected workspace. The structure of backend-related data, such as tables and Edge Functions, is copied over without any information. For any automatically generated keys in the original project, Lovable generates fresh keys in the remixed project. Manually added keys are not copied over.</span><span data-as="p">When remixing, you can select which region to host the new project’s backend (Americas, Europe, or Asia Pacific). Lovable preselects the region closest to you based on your location. Once selected, the region cannot be changed for that project.</span><span data-as="p">Keep in mind that in a remixed Cloud project you can’t <a class="link" href="/features/projects/history">revert</a> to versions from before the remix. Versions created after the remix revert normally.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-supabase-to-cloud-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-supabase-to-cloud" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-supabase-to-cloud-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I migrate my project from Supabase to Cloud?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-supabase-to-cloud-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-supabase-to-cloud-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">At the moment, migration from Supabase to Cloud is not supported. If your project is already connected to Supabase, we recommend continuing to use it, as we will keep supporting Supabase moving forward.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-cloud-to-my-own-supabase-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-cloud-to-my-own-supabase" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-cloud-to-my-own-supabase-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I migrate my project from Cloud to my own Supabase?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-cloud-to-my-own-supabase-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-migrate-my-project-from-cloud-to-my-own-supabase-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">There is no one-click migration from the built-in backend (Cloud) to your own Supabase project. If you want to move, you export your Cloud data, connect a Supabase project to a new Lovable project, and rebuild the schema there. Remixing a Cloud project produces another Cloud project, so it does not switch the backend for you. See <a class="link" href="/features/advanced-settings#export-lovable-cloud-data">Export Lovable Cloud data</a> and <a class="link" href="/integrations/supabase">Connect your own Supabase project</a> for the steps.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 [&amp;&gt;div]:ml-2 [&amp;&gt;div]:mr-0 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="can-i-set-a-default-region-for-all-new-projects-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="can-i-set-a-default-region-for-all-new-projects" class="absolute top-[-8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="can-i-set-a-default-region-for-all-new-projects-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Can I set a default region for all new projects?</p></div></summary><div id="can-i-set-a-default-region-for-all-new-projects-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-set-a-default-region-for-all-new-projects-accordion-title" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">Yes. You can set a preferred default region for new Cloud projects: open <strong>Connectors</strong>, select <strong>Cloud</strong>, and choose a region under <strong>Where projects are hosted</strong>.</span><span data-as="p">On <strong>Business and Enterprise</strong> workspaces, admins can also set a required default region for every new project from <a href="https://lovable.dev/settings/privacy-security" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Privacy &amp; security</a> (<strong>Settings → Security → Privacy &amp; security → Default hosting region</strong>). When this workspace-level default is set, members cannot choose a different region when creating a project. See <a class="link" href="/features/privacy-and-security-settings#default-hosting-region">Default hosting region</a> for more information.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full
